           1 13:13:35      Indeed, it had been devised by somebody called

          13 13:14:19      inform DIEZA either that those proposals had

          15 13:14:26           Instead, for its own commercial reasons,
          16 13:14:28      it engaged a subcontractor called AEH.  But
          17 13:14:34      the difficulty was the two products were
          18 13:14:36      fundamentally different.  Whereas the Onlyglass
          19 13:14:40      product involved a strip of LEDs running in air
          20 13:14:48      between two planes of glass, the design that
          21 13:14:52      had been adopted by AEH involved the LED,
          22 13:14:57      the pixels, being affixed to a pane of glass,
